southeast.

JH> help verbs
Here are some verbs you might try while playing Legends:

act      advance    applaud   arm       assist    avoid   backstab
balance  bark       berserk   bite      blend     blink   blush
bow      break      brief     buy       call      cast    chuckle
chant    claw       climb     close     cough     curtsy  defensive
depart   deposit    descend   disarm    disband   drink   disguise
drop     eat        echo      emote     enroll    fill    follow
forget   frenzy     frown     gasp      gesture   grimace grin
go       gulp       handraise handshake headshake health  hide
hold     howl       ignite    inventory join      kick    kneel
knock    latch      laugh     lay       lean      load    mana
mark     massage    moderate  mold      mutter    nibble  nod
open     psi        pay       pick      pour      power   pull
punch    put        recall    release   remove    roar    salute
split    stalk      stand     status    steal     submit  swoon
take     tap        tickle    toast     transform turn    verbose
wealth   wear       whisper   withdraw  yell

This is by no means a complete list. Legends understands over four hundred
different verbs. Feel free to experiment!

[Level One, Stairway]
A grand staircase dominates this area, formed of alternating sections of
white and black crystal. The banisters have veins of some intricately 
woven icy metal passing through the very centers. At the base of the stairs
two large pillars of shimmering silver crystal rise upwards. 
You see Warsprite, Emanon, Firestar, Nord and Chaya. 
Obvious exits: north, south.

stairway is visible leading up to the dais in the center of the area.

[Level One, Corner]
This is an oddly shaped rectangular hallway with sparkling solid crystal 
walls along three sides. The shorter northern wall is possibly ten feet 
high, while the other two are closer to thirty. The rest of the northern 
area is open leading to another open area. Torches line the walls at even
increments. A small crystal pedestal rests in the southeastern corner.
A darkened square of crystal marrs the floor near the southern wall.
You see Chaya, a coldfire bat (imprisoned), a coldfire bat (imprisoned) and a
coldfire bat (imprisoned). A ladder is there.

This dark, chill stone chamber is dominated by a granite staircase leading 
upwards into the darkness. The northern wall is lined with chain after chain
attached to neck and wrist shackles. On the floor are shorter chains, only a 
foot or so in length attached to bolts on the floor.
You see Warsprite, Emanon, Yourion (dead), Firestar, Nord, Chaya, an ice lich
(dead), a death warrior, a winter wight and an ice lich (dead). A copper coin
is here.

This dark, chill stone chamber is dominated by a granite staircase leading 
upwards into the darkness. The northern wall is lined with chain after chain

[Level Three, Chamber]
This drab stone chamber is cold, the walls seem to leech out the
warmth of the air. Chains dangle from the walls to fetter those
awaiting their turn under the massive weights. On the floor are 
long rectangular sections of stone, marked out by the bolts at the
corners. Along the wall are various sizes and shapes of weights, from
small hand held ones, to large blocks of stone. Several torches
hang from the walls.
